# The offsets from UTC of the zones (by their names as ICU keeps them)
# through their history, as the reference browser's ICU (Chrome 156,
# ICU 78) has them in its zoneinfo64: zone; its first offset in
# seconds (before any change), then
# each change as the seconds since the one before (since 1970 for the
# first) and the offset from then, in base 36 apart by /, + after one
# of daylight time; then its final rule, the offset it keeps the rule
# from (base 36) and the year it does from. * and a rule: ICU's
# SimpleTimeZone rule, the start's month (from 0), day, weekday and
# seconds and how they are told (0 wall, 1 standard, 2 UTC), the end's
# likewise, and the daylight saving in seconds.
